PART ni— SECTION 4 
Miscellaneous Notifications 


RESERVE BANK OF INDIA 


NOTIFICATION 

Bombay 1, the 20 th February 1954 

No. 2“On the expiry of his deputation with the Gov- 
ernment of India;, Ministry of Finance, Shri B. R. Shenoy 
reverted to the Reserve Bank of India with effect from 
the 1st December 1953 and is posted as Director of Rural 
Economics, Reserve Bank of India, Department of Re- 
search and Statistics, Bombay, with effect from the 14th 
January 1954. 

2. Shri M. J. Ambani, Banking Officer, Reserve Bank 
of India, Department of Banking Operations, Calcutta 
was granted leave from the 19th December 1953 to the 
16th January 1954. 

3. Shri B. K. Mukerji, officiating Assistant Currency 
Officer, Reserve Bank of India, Issue Department, Calcutta 
reverted as Superintendent with effect from the 15th 
January 1954. 

4. Shri V. N. Vinekar is appointed to officiate as Bank- 
ing Officer, Reserve Bank of India, Department of Bank- 
ing Operations, Madras with effect from the 15th Janu- 
ary 1954 vice Shri P. R. Sethi proceeded on leave. 

5. Shri E. J. D’Costa, officiating Assistant Accountant, 
Reserve Bank of India, Public Debt Office, Madras re- 
verted as Sub- Accountant with effect from the close of 
business on the 16th January 1954. 

6. On return from leave Shri D. Maitra is reposted as 
Banking Officer, Reserve Bank of India, Department, of 
Banking Operations, Calcutta with effect from the 18th 
January 1954. 

7. Shri S. B. Banerjee is appointed to officiate as As- 
sistant Accountant, Reserve Bank of India. Calcutta with 
effect from the close of business on the 20th January 1954 
vice Shri N. B. Sen. 

8. Shri N. B. Sen is posted as Assistant Accountant, Re- 
serve Bank of India, Public Debt Office, Calcutta with 
effect from the close of business on the 22nd January 
1954 vice Shri N. N. Roy 

9. Shri B, K. Mukerji is re-appointed to officiate as As- 
sistant Currency Officer, Reserve Bank of India, Issue De- 
partment, Calcutta, with effect from the 26th January 
1954. 

10. Shri L. T, Moras, Currency Officer, Reserve Bank of 
India, Issue Department, Delhi is appointed to officiate 
as Manager, Reserve Bank of India. Delhi with effect from 
the 27th January 1954 vice Shri A. L, Quamarain expired 
on the same date. 

11. Shri P, C. Sood, Accountant, Reserve Bank of India, 
Publid Debt Office, Delhi is posted as Currency Officer, 
Reserve Bank of India, Issue Department, Delhi, with 
effect from the close of business on the 27th January 1954 
vice Shri L. T. Moras. 

12. Shri P. K. Lahiri, Assistant Accountant, Reserve 
Bank of India, Public Debt Office, Delhi is appointed as 
Accountant in the same office with effect from the close 
of business on the 27th January 1954 vice Shri P, C. Sood. 

13. The services of Shri T. Satyanarayana Rao, Assistant 
Chief Officer, Reserve Bank of India, Department of Bank- 
ing Development, Bombay have been placed at the dis- 
posal of the Government of India, Planning Commission, 
with effect from the close of business on the 30th January 
1954. 


14. Shri N. N, Roy is appointed to officiate as Accountant, 
Reserve Bank of India, Calcutta with effect from the close 
of business on the 30th January 1954 vice Shri B. N. Dutt 
proceeded on leave preparatory to retirement. 

15. Shri P. Karunakaran is appointed to officiate as Cur- 
rency Officer, Reserve Bank of India, Issue Department, 
Bombay with effect from the close of business on the 30th 
January 1954, vice Shri A. K. Banerjee proceeded on leave 
preparatory to retirement. 

16. Shri A. R. Thanawala, Deputy Chief Officer, Reserve 
Bank of India, Department of Banking Operations, Bom- 
bay proceeded on leave with effect from the 1st February 
1954. 

17. Shri G. Pratapchandran is appointed to officiate as 
Banking Officer. Reserve Bank of India, Department of 
Banking Operations. Bombay with effect from the 1st 
February 1954 nice Shri M. L. Gogtay. 

18. Shri M. L, Gogtay is appointed as Assistant Chief 
Officer, Reserve Bank of India, Department of Banking 
Operations, Bombay with effect from the 1st February 
1954. 

19. On return from leave Shri S. T. Karnik is reposted 
as Banking Officer, Reserve Bank of India, Department 
of Banking Operations, Madras with effect from the 2nd 
February 1954 vice Shri D. Lakshminarayan reverted as 
Superintendent. 

20. On return from leave Shri K. S. Jodhka is posted 
as Assistant Currency Officer. Reserve Bank of India, 
Issue Department, Kanpur with effect from the close of 
business on the 6th February 1954 vice Shri K. Achan 
transferred. 

21. Shri N. Krishnamurti, Accountant, Reserve Bank of 
India. Central Office. Bombay proceeded on' leave with 
effect from the 8th February 1954. 

22. Shri Balwant Singh is appointed to officiate as As- 
sistant Accountant, Reserve Bank of India, Public Debt 
Office, Delhi, with effect from the 9th February 1954. 

23. .On return from leave Shri G. S. Annaswami is post- 
ed as Banking Officer. Reserve Bank of India, Department 
of Banking Operations. Trivandrum with effect from the 
10th February 1954 vice Shri R. N. Majumdar reverted 
as Staff Assistant. 

24. On return from leave Shri V. B. Das is reposted as 
Assistant Accountant. Reserve Bank of India, Central 
Office. Bombay with effect from the 11th February 1954. 

25. Shri I. D. Chhabra is appointed to officiate as As- 
sistant Accountant, Reserve Bank of India, Bombay, with 
effect from the close of business on the 11th February 1954 
vice Shri V. S, Patwardhan. 

26. Shri V. S. Patwardhan is posted as officiating Ac- 
countant, Reserve Bank of India, Bombay with effect from 
the dose of business on the 11th February 1954 vice Shri 
S. K. Banerjee transferred. 

27. Shri R, R. Kalke is appointed to officiate as Deputy 
Chief Accountant, Reserve Bank of India, Central Office, 
Bombay with effect from the 12th February 1954 vice 
Shri R, C. Sachdeva placed on special duty with effect 
from the 10th February 1954. 

28. Shri S. Narayanswamy is posted as Assistant Chief 
Accountant, Reserve Bank of India, Central Office, Bombay 
with effect from the 12th February 1954 vice Shri R, R. 
Kalke. 

N, SUNDARESAN 

Deputy Governor
